We’ll take the Under based on head-to-head trends along with the Sixers’ 2026 betting data, while the Pistons to cover also has legs based on the statistics.

## Game Betting Analysis

It’s hard to go against the home Pistons in this one, as they are one of the best ATS squads in the NBA with a 6-1 record while covering the last four games against Philadelphia – also going back to six of eight meetings. Detroit has also covered the spread in five straight games as the favorite, winning six outright in a row against the Sixers. However, Philadelphia enters with a respectable 5-3 ATS mark while Joel Embiid might actually play. This concerns us slightly so we head to the total market instead where the Sixers are 2-6 favoring the Under mightily. The Under has hit in four of five meetings along with six of the last eight head-to-heads.

* * *

## 76ers-Pistons Odds (Spread, Moneyline, Over/Under)

## Deciding Factor: Paul Reed’s Stretch-Five Breakout vs. Joel Embiid’s 25-Minute Ceiling

Detroit has been without Jalen Duren since he strained his right hamstring 10 minutes into the October 23 win over Miami, and while the team said he’d be out “at least two weeks,” he’s still not back for Wednesday’s rematch. Paul Reed has filled the gap capably rather than just adequately: since stepping into the void, he’s averaging 14 points, 7 rebounds and 2 assists while shooting better than 70% from three on nearly two attempts a night, giving Cade Cunningham a frontcourt partner who spaces the floor rather than just rolling to the rim. Reed flashed exactly that ceiling the night Duren went down, finishing 8-for-11 from the field (2-for-2 from deep) for 19 points and 11 rebounds, and his craftiness finishing around length has offset what Detroit’s missing inside. The results speak for themselves — the Pistons are 6-1, winners of three straight, and have held four consecutive opponents under 95 points, a defensive floor that travels regardless of who’s protecting the rim and gives Detroit a real chance to control tempo again tonight.

Philadelphia, meanwhile, got good news when Joel Embiid didn’t appear on Wednesday’s injury report and is expected to play at Little Caesars Arena, but coach Nick Nurse has him on a firm 25-minute restriction while managing a right plantar fasciitis issue that had Embiid sitting the entire fourth quarter of Monday’s win over Chicago. His per-game numbers are still strong — 19.2 points, 9.4 rebounds and 5.2 assists through five games — but that workload cap means extended stretches without his two-way anchor, and Philadelphia’s depth behind him is thin, with Dominick Barlow (low back stress reaction) out since early camp and Ariel Hukporti done for the season after Achilles surgery. If Reed can keep knocking down threes and cleaning the glass in those center minutes whenever Embiid sits, Detroit’s switch-heavy, possession-starving defense could turn this into the same kind of slog that bit Philadelphia in the October 25 meeting — giving the Pistons a genuine path to a season-series sweep even with their own starting center still sidelined

* * *

Randle has been a seamless fit in Brooklyn since Opening Night, delivering 48 points + rebounds + assists over 38 minutes against the same Hornets squad he’ll face Wednesday right out of the gate on Opening Night. The veteran big man is averaging 20.9 points, 7.7 rebounds and 4.9 assists per contest while shooting an aytpically poor 41.5%, meaning there’s actually still plenty room for a boost in his offensive numbers.

Randle now gets another crack at Charlotte on Wednesday, and although the Hornets have some solid defensive metrics in certain categories, their work against power forwards speaks to plenty of vulnerability. The Hornets have allowed an Eastern Conference-high 31.8 offensive efficiency rating to fours, along with 49.0% shooting, including 40.8% from three-point range, to the position.

Charlotte is also surrendering the fifth-most rebounds per game to PFs (11.5), and Randle has come just a single point or rebound short of going 5-2 to the Over on this prop thus far this season.

**Original Line Publish:** 25.5

Williamson is the second of our three bigs who we like the outlook for Wednesday, and the 2019 first overall pick is off to a hot start in his own right with averages of 26.6 points + rebounds across 32.1 minutes per game. As customary, Zion is doing the overwhelming majority of his damage from in front of the arc, draining 63.6% of his shots on 12.6 field-goal attempts per game, with only 1.0 per contest coming from behind the arc.

Zion has averaged 28.5 points + rebounds per contest against Utah over 15 games in his career, including a 28 points + rebounds tally versus the Jazz in the second game of the season this year. Then, the Jazz are allowing the eighth-most points per game to power forwards (26.6), and Utah is also facilitating the fourth-most free-throw attempts per contest to fours (7.9).

Like Randle, Zion is just a point or rebound short of having cashed the Over in five of seven games so far in the new campaign, and New Orleans’ 120.5-point implied team total also adds appeal to the Over.

**Original Line Publish**: 25.5

[![Image 46: Polymarket](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/sportsbooks/square/polymarket.svg)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/661009/polymarket_sc)[O/U Total Rebounds (@ SAC)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/661009/polymarket_sc)[9.5](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/661009/polymarket_sc)

[![Image 47: Kalshi](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/sportsbooks/square/kalshi.svg)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_blocks/661009/kalshi_sc)[O/U Total Blocks (@ SAC)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_blocks/661009/kalshi_sc)[1.5](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_blocks/661009/kalshi_sc)

[![Image 48](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/logo-watermark.svg)](https://www.metabet.io/?utm_source=lineups&utm_medium=adtile-watermark&utm_campaign=nba/walker_kessler/player_174656 "Powered by MetaBet")[](https://www.metabet.io/odds)

Kessler, like Randle, is a new frontcourt piece to his team that’s paid instant dividends. The 2022 first-round pick only played five games during his final season in Utah in 2025-26, but now that he’s back to full health, Kessler has been the force the Lakers were hoping for when they let the defensively challenged Deandre Ayton walk this offseason. Kessler is delivering a well-rounded 14.4 points, 10.5 rebounds, 1.3 assists, 2.5 blocks and 1.1 steals per contest while shooting 71.4%, his highest figure since his rookie season.

Kessler has a premium position matchup on tap Wednesday as well, considering the Kings have hands-down been among the worst teams against centers in almost every conceivable metric. Sacramento is allowing an NBA-high offensive efficiency rating to centers (37.4), along with NBA-high 67.8% shooting to the position. The Kings are also yielding an NBA-high 27.9 points per contest to fives and an NBA-high 56.6 points in the paint per contest, while Kessler is scoring a career-high 87.0% of his points in that part of the floor.

**Original Line Publish**: 22.5

Brunson’s not quite out of his season-opening shooting slump yet, but there’s seemingly some light at the end of the tunnel. After draining just 36.6% of his attempts over the first four games of the season, Brunson went 13-for-25 from the floor in a win over the Bulls on Wednesday. He regressed to an 8-for-22 tally in a tough matchup against the Sixers on Friday, and after having the weekend to clear his head, Brunson should be refreshed for a favorable matchup with the intracity-rival Nets.

Brooklyn is allowing the fifth-most assists per game to point guards (10.8), along with 46.7% shooting overall. The Nets are also yielding the seventh-most assists per contest (28.2), and he’s come just two total points or assists short of going 4-0 to the Over on this prop in the last four games. Finally, it’s worth noting the Nets have yielded the eighth-most points + assists per game across all positions (144.2), giving further fuel to the Over.

**Original Line Publish:** 30.5

The Mavericks are nearly double-digit favorites Monday against Sacramento, and given the winless Kings have lost their games by an average of 19 points, it’s possible Dallas is ahead by a comfortable enough margin late that a front-line player like Cooper Flagg with elevated props doesn’t get quite enough playing time to hit them. That makes Washington an interesting pivot, considering his points + rebounds prop is very reasonable in this matchup and relative to the veteran forward’s body fo work thus far.

Washington is averaging 22.0 points + rebounds across 33.5 minutes per game while going 3-2 to the Over on the 21.5 over the first five games of the season while falling short in tough matchups against the Rockets and Spurs. Washington then managed an outlier of a three-point effort in a rematch with Houston his most recent time on the floor, but he’ll have his most palatable matchup by far Monday. With Keegan Murray (ankle) sidelined, Sacramento is more vulnerable down low and is ranked No. 22 in offensive efficiency rating allowed to power forwards (29.5), while also surrendering the sixth-most points per game to the position (26.8). The Kings are also yielding the seventh-most rebounds per game overall (56.5), along with NBA-high 55.3% shooting on the road.

**Original Line Publish**: 21.5

[![Image 45](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/logo-watermark.svg)](https://www.metabet.io/?utm_source=lineups&utm_medium=adtile-watermark&utm_campaign=nba/victor_wembanyama/player_188716 "Powered by MetaBet")[](https://www.metabet.io/odds)

Wemby has taken his already elite game to new heights over the last pair of contests, averaging 39.5 points, 10.5 rebounds, 4.5 assists and 6.0 steals + blocks across 36.5 minutes per contest. The majority of the 2023 first overall pick’s props are fairly elevated as a result, and that’s a bit of a concern considering the Spurs are 16.5-point favorites against the 1-5 Jazz and could therefore give some of their top players a bit of down time if the game plays out as oddsmakers expect.

However, we can still take advantage of what should be a prime matchup for the big man by focusing on his steals + blocks prop, which is reasonable given both his body of work and the matchup. Wemby is already averaging 5.5 steals + blocks per contest, and he’s now facing a Utah team that’s allowed the third-most steals + blocks per contest (15.7) and 4.0 steals + blocks per game to centers specifically.

Wembanyama has also enjoyed plenty of success in these two categories during his 10 career games against Utah, averaging 5.8 steals + blocks and going a pristine 10-0 to the Over on the 3.5 number.

**Original Line Publish**: 3.5

# NBA Player Props Today: Pascal Siakam, Rui Hachimura and Austin Reaves Best Bets

 Check out the best NBA player prop bets and picks for today’s games featuring Pascal Siakam, Rui Hachimura and Austin Reaves plays.

Let’s dive into three player props that catch our eye for Sunday’s action-packed ledger. Be sure to[check out the NBA betting trends page](https://www.lineups.com/nba/odds/betting-trends/)for odds, data and insights on every game.

## **Best NBA Sunday Props**

*   Pascal Siakam Over 30.5 Points + Rebounds
*   Rui Hachimura Over 13.5 Points
*   Austin Reaves Over 19.5 Points

### **Pascal Siakam (Pacers vs. Nets) Over Points + Rebounds**

Siakam let us down in a favorable matchup against the Heat on Friday night, but there are several reasons to go right back to the well with the star big man on Sunday. It begins with the fact the outing against Miami was a true outlier, as Siakam went just 5-for-17 from the field after entering the game having shot 55.3% over the first five contests of the season. Siakam’s rebounds were also down to five despite Ivica Zubac’s absence.

Both Zubac (ankle) and Tyrese Haliburton (hamstring) will remain sidelined Sunday, and Siakam has a 30.2% usage rate and averages of a team-high 25.9 points along with 7.3 rebounds per 36 minutes with both teammates off the floor. Then, the Nets are allowing the seventh-highest offensive efficiency rating to power forwards (30.4), along with the fifth-most points per road contest (125.0). Brooklyn is also conceding 47.7% shooting on the road, and Siakam cashed the Over on this prop in three of the first five games before the dud against Miami.

**Original Line Publish:** 30.5

### **Rui Hachimura (Clippers vs. Warriors) Over Points**

Hachimura is, at least on paper, arguably the Cliippers’ first unit’s second-best scoring option behind Darius Garland. However, the floor-stretching big man, who came into the season having averaged 12.6 points per game on 49.9% shooting over the first seven seasons of his career, hasn’t come close to hitting his stride offensively. Hachimura’s trademark efficiency has taken a dive early in his Clippers tenure, as he’s drained an abysmal 28.6% of his shots overall. However, he’s actually been markedly more successful from long range with a 33.3% figure in that split, meaning he’s simply run into some very bad luck when putting the ball up in front of the arc.

A shooter as proven as Hachimura is going to bounce back sooner rather than later, and a matchup against a Warriors squad that’s improving but has still had some trouble defensively could offer an opportunity for at least a slight uptick offensively. Golden State has surrendered 47.4% shooting to power forwards, including 41.0% from long range. The Dubs are also yielding the second-highest shooting percentage overall (49.3%), and Hachimura’s current average of 12.3 field-goal attempts per game is a career high and reflective of the bigger role he’s already filling in his new digs.

**Original Line Publish**: 13.5

### **Austin Reaves (Lakers vs. Raptors) Over Points**

Reaves is off to an impressively efficient start as a scorer this season, averaging a career-high 23.8 points on career-best 54.8% shooting, including a career-best 42.4% from downtown. The talented two-guard has delivered at least 21 points in all but one game in the early going, and he’s averaging a robust 21.0 field-goal + free-throw attempts per contest as Luka Doncic’s literal and figurative wing man.

Reaves’ Sunday matchup comes against a Raptors team that’s been somewhat erratic over the first two weeks of the season. Toronto opened 2-0 before dropping two of its subsequent three games, giving up 117 and 121 points in that pair of defeats. The Raptors have had trouble defending two-guards in particular, allowing the 10th-highest offensive efficiency rating to the position (26.0), as well as 37.7% three-point shooting to the position.

Toronto is also facilitating a five-percent boost in opponent shooting on the road (46.8%) as compared to at home (41.4%), including a massive 9.2% increase in three-point shooting (39.7%, compared to 30.5% at home). We’re still dealing with very small samples overall this early, but the numbers and this scoring threshold both point toward the Over.

**Original Line Publish**: 19.5

## **Best NBA Saturday Props**

*   Cedric Coward Over 22.5 Points + Rebounds
*   Ajay Mitchell Over 25.5 Points + Rebounds + Assists

### **Onyeka Okongwu (Hawks vs. Pelicans) Over Points + Rebounds**

[](https://www.lineups.com/)

![Image 31](https://metabet.static.areyouwatchingthis.com/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/images/teams/80x80/10187.png)Onyeka Okongwu

[![Image 32: Kalshi](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/sportsbooks/square/kalshi.svg)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_points/660998/kalshi_sc)[O/U Points Scored (@ WAS)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_points/660998/kalshi_sc)[14.5](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_points/660998/kalshi_sc)

[![Image 33: Polymarket](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/sportsbooks/square/polymarket.svg)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/660998/polymarket_sc)[O/U Total Rebounds (@ WAS)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/660998/polymarket_sc)[7.5](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/660998/polymarket_sc)

[![Image 34: Polymarket](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/sportsbooks/square/polymarket.svg)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_assists/660998/polymarket_sc)[O/U Total Assists (@ WAS)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_assists/660998/polymarket_sc)[1.5](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_assists/660998/polymarket_sc)

[![Image 35](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/logo-watermark.svg)](https://www.metabet.io/?utm_source=lineups&utm_medium=adtile-watermark&utm_campaign=nba/onyeka_okongwu/player_135190 "Powered by MetaBet")[](https://www.metabet.io/odds)

Okongwu’s stat lines have been all over the map early this season, but the versatile veteran big man is certainly capable of eclipsing 20.5 points + rebounds in the right matchup and with enough playing time. In fact, Okongwu just blew past this number his last time on the floor in a tough on-paper matchup against the Cavaliers on Thursday, posting 41 points + rebounds across 33 minutes on an 11-for-17 tally from the floor, including a 4-for-6 showing from behind the arc.

Okongwu now has an exponentially better scenario in front of him Saturday against the Pelicans, which have surrendered an NBA-high 39.8 offensive efficiency rating to centers, along with surrendering an NBA-high 27.0 points and third-most rebounds (16.4) per game to the position. The Pels are also giving up 57.5% shooting to fives, and although Okongwu spaces the floor with regularity, it’s still worth noting New Orleans has yielded the third-most points in the paint per game (55.6), while 57.1% of Okongwu’s scoring so far has come in that part of the floor.

**Original Line Publish:** 20.5

### **Cedric Coward (Grizzlies vs. Kings) Over Points + Rebounds**

[](https://www.lineups.com/)

![Image 36](https://metabet.static.areyouwatchingthis.com/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/images/teams/80x80/10206.png)Cedric Coward

[![Image 37: Underdog](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/sportsbooks/square/underdog.svg)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_points/661002/underdog_sc)[O/U Points Scored (vs. IND)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_points/661002/underdog_sc)[12.5](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_points/661002/underdog_sc)

[![Image 38: Underdog](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/sportsbooks/square/underdog.svg)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/661002/underdog_sc)[O/U Total Rebounds (vs. IND)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/661002/underdog_sc)[6.5](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/661002/underdog_sc)

[![Image 39: Kalshi](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/sportsbooks/square/kalshi.svg)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_assists/661002/kalshi_sc)[O/U Total Assists (vs. IND)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_assists/661002/kalshi_sc)[1.5](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_assists/661002/kalshi_sc)

[![Image 40](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/logo-watermark.svg)](https://www.metabet.io/?utm_source=lineups&utm_medium=adtile-watermark&utm_campaign=nba/cedric_coward/player_258753 "Powered by MetaBet")[](https://www.metabet.io/odds)

Coward has kicked off his second NBA season in fine form, averaging 14.3 points on 51.1% shooting, including an incandescently hot 68.8% from three-point range. Coward is supplementing the solid scoring average with 9.3 rebounds, 2.5 assists and 1.3 steals per contest, and Saturday, he’ll be in a highly appealing matchup while potentially operating without his high-usage backcourt mate, Ty Jerome, who’s questionable due to a lingering back issue.

Coward has averaged 30.6 points + rebounds per 36 minutes when not sharing the floor with Jerome so far in the new season. Coward’s positional matchup against the Kings is also a premium one, as Sacramento is surrendering the fifth-highest offensive efficiency rating to two-guards (29.2) and 50.6% shooting to the position. The Kings are also yielding the eighth-most rebounds per game (56.4) and sixth-highest defensive rebounding rate (75.6%), while Coward is averaging a career-high 8.0 defensive rebounds per contest.

**Original Line Publish**: 22.5

### **Ajay Mitchell (Thunder vs. Rockets) Over**Points + Rebounds + Assists

[](https://www.lineups.com/)

![Image 41](https://metabet.static.areyouwatchingthis.com/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/images/teams/80x80/15673.png)Ajay Mitchell

[![Image 42: Underdog](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/sportsbooks/square/underdog.svg)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_points/661004/underdog_sc)[O/U Points Scored (vs. CLE)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_points/661004/underdog_sc)[14.5](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_points/661004/underdog_sc)

[![Image 43: Polymarket](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/sportsbooks/square/polymarket.svg)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/661004/polymarket_sc)[O/U Total Rebounds (vs. CLE)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/661004/polymarket_sc)[3.5](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/661004/polymarket_sc)

[![Image 44: Polymarket](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/sportsbooks/square/polymarket.svg)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_assists/661004/polymarket_sc)[O/U Total Assists (vs. CLE)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_assists/661004/polymarket_sc)[3.5](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_assists/661004/polymarket_sc)

[![Image 45](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/logo-watermark.svg)](https://www.metabet.io/?utm_source=lineups&utm_medium=adtile-watermark&utm_campaign=nba/ajay_mitchell/player_207753 "Powered by MetaBet")[](https://www.metabet.io/odds)

Mitchell has been outstanding in the wake of the Thunder’s early-season injury plague, and with Chet Holmgren now being ruled out for multiple weeks due to a patellar tendon tear, there will be even more usage available for Mitchell to absorb. The 2024 second-round pick has worked as a starter over the last two games and thrived, averaging 36.5 points + rebounds + assists while shooting a blistering 61.1% overall. Mitchell has stayed aggressive as well, averaging 24.5 field-goal + free-throw attempts per game in that span.

Mitchell sports a 29.3% usage rate and is averaging 30.3 points + rebounds + assists per 36 minutes this season when all of Holmgren, Jalen Williams (leg) and Cason Wallace (shoulder) have been off the floor this season. Mitchell is also seeing a +9.0-point increase per 36 minutes when not sharing the court with his three teammates, and over their first five games, the Rockets have allowed 48.8% shooting to small forwards, along with the 11th-most points per game to the position (22.6).

**Original Line Publish**:25.5

## **Best NBA Friday Props**

*   Derrick White Over 23.5 Points + Rebounds + Assists
*   Pascal Siakam Over 31.5 Points + Rebounds
*   Cooper Flagg Over 35.5 Points + Rebounds + Assists

### **Derrick White (Celtics vs. Bulls) Over Points + Rebounds + Assists**

[](https://www.lineups.com/)

![Image 31](https://metabet.static.areyouwatchingthis.com/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/images/teams/80x80/10189.png)Derrick White

[![Image 32: Kalshi](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/sportsbooks/square/kalshi.svg)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_points/660999/kalshi_sc)[O/U Points Scored (vs. MIL)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_points/660999/kalshi_sc)[14.5](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_points/660999/kalshi_sc)

[![Image 33: Polymarket](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/sportsbooks/square/polymarket.svg)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/660999/polymarket_sc)[O/U Total Rebounds (vs. MIL)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/660999/polymarket_sc)[3.5](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/660999/polymarket_sc)

[![Image 34: Underdog](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/sportsbooks/square/underdog.svg)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_assists/660999/underdog_sc)[O/U Total Assists (vs. MIL)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_assists/660999/underdog_sc)[4.5](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_assists/660999/underdog_sc)

[![Image 35](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/logo-watermark.svg)](https://www.metabet.io/?utm_source=lineups&utm_medium=adtile-watermark&utm_campaign=nba/derrick_white/player_69913 "Powered by MetaBet")[](https://www.metabet.io/odds)

White is off to a typically solid start, and although he’s seen a downturn in scoring early in the new season thanks to a drop in usage, he’s partly offsetting that with more efficient shooting when he does put the ball up. The veteran point guard is draining 44.2% of his shots from the field thus far, a middling figure that’s nevertheless an improvement on the career-worst 39.4% he shot last season.

White is also facilitating at a career-best clip, averaging a career-high 6.3 assists per contest while also pulling down 4.0 rebounds per game. He took only four shots against the Bulls at United Center on Monday, but he’s otherwise put up at least 11 shot attempts in Boston’s other three contests. Chicago does present a very appealing matchup on paper as well, allowing an NBA-high 38.4 offensive efficiency rating to point guards, along with 50.5% shooting, including an NBA-high 53.2% success rate from three-point range.

White is 2-2 to the Over on this prop thus far, but the home matchup against such a vulnerable backcourt sets him up for a good shot at cashing this prop.

**Original Line Publish:** 23.5

### **Pascal Siakam (Pacers vs. Heat) Over Points + Rebounds**

[](https://www.lineups.com/)

![Image 36](https://metabet.static.areyouwatchingthis.com/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/images/teams/80x80/10201.png)Pascal Siakam

[![Image 37: Underdog](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/sportsbooks/square/underdog.svg)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_points/661002/underdog_sc)[O/U Points Scored (@ MEM)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_points/661002/underdog_sc)[23.5](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_points/661002/underdog_sc)

[![Image 38: Kalshi](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/sportsbooks/square/kalshi.svg)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/661002/kalshi_sc)[O/U Total Rebounds (@ MEM)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/661002/kalshi_sc)[5.5](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/661002/kalshi_sc)

[![Image 39: Polymarket](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/sportsbooks/square/polymarket.svg)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_assists/661002/polymarket_sc)[O/U Total Assists (@ MEM)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_assists/661002/polymarket_sc)[3.5](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_assists/661002/polymarket_sc)

[![Image 40](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/logo-watermark.svg)](https://www.metabet.io/?utm_source=lineups&utm_medium=adtile-watermark&utm_campaign=nba/pascal_siakam/player_56163 "Powered by MetaBet")[](https://www.metabet.io/odds)

Siakam is once again spearheading the Pacers’ efforts in the first week-plus of the new season, averaging a typically well-rounded 24.0 points (on career-best 55.3% shooting), 8.4 rebounds and 3.8 assists per contest. Siakam is shooting 38.1% from three-point range as well, displaying his usual floor-spacing prowess. He now draws a matchup against a Heat team that finally just won its first game, but that’s struggled defensively pretty much across the board.

Miami is allowing 124.0 points per home game over its three Kaseya Center contests thus far, as well as 48.4% shooting. The Heat also ranks in the bottom 10 of the league in rebounds (59.0) and assists (28.3) per home game surrendered, and Siakam also had PRA tallies of 45 and 47 in two of three regular-season games against Miami last season.

To top it off, the Heat is surrendering a league-high 52.6% success from rate from deep to power forwards over its first five game, giving the Over even more appeal in this case.

**Original Line Publish**: 31.5

### **Cooper Flagg (Mavericks vs. Rockets) Over Points + Rebounds + Assists**

[](https://www.lineups.com/)

![Image 41](https://metabet.static.areyouwatchingthis.com/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/images/teams/80x80/10194.png)Cooper Flagg

[![Image 42: Polymarket](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/sportsbooks/square/polymarket.svg)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_points/661008/polymarket_sc)[O/U Points Scored (@ SAN)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_points/661008/polymarket_sc)[24.5](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_points/661008/polymarket_sc)

[![Image 43: Kalshi](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/sportsbooks/square/kalshi.svg)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/661008/kalshi_sc)[O/U Total Rebounds (@ SAN)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/661008/kalshi_sc)[5.5](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_rebounds/661008/kalshi_sc)

[![Image 44: Polymarket](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/sportsbooks/square/polymarket.svg)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_assists/661008/polymarket_sc)[O/U Total Assists (@ SAN)](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_assists/661008/polymarket_sc)[5.5](https://lineups.go.metabet.io/bet/nba_game_player_assists/661008/polymarket_sc)

[![Image 45](https://go.metabet.io/03325fb169d4ca908f52c07a4505bb8f/img/logo-watermark.svg)](https://www.metabet.io/?utm_source=lineups&utm_medium=adtile-watermark&utm_campaign=nba/cooper_flagg/player_258778 "Powered by MetaBet")[](https://www.metabet.io/odds)

Fresh off his Rookie of the year honors, Flagg already looks like a leading MVP candidate through his first four games. Heading into the season, the prevailing thought was that finally having the opportunity to play alongside Kyrie Irving was going to benefit Flagg in a multitude of ways, and Flagg’s current line of 25.3 points (on 52.2% shooting), 6.8 assists, 5.3 rebounds, 2.8 steals and 1.3 blocks per contest certainly seems to bear that out.

Flagg should be set for another busy night Friday against the intrastate-rival Rockets, who he already hung 39 points + rebounds + assists on over 33 minutes back on Opening Night, a game in which he sent 10-for-17 from the field and 11-for-13 from the free-throw line. The Rockets also rank No. 19 in offensive efficiency rating allowed to small forwards (23.6) and are surrendering 48.8% shooting to the position, and Flagg also had one effort of 51 points + rebounds + assists against Houston last season as a rookie without Irving around to draw defensive attention.

Dallas also has an implied team total of 117 points for Friday’s game, another sign Flagg should have plenty of opportunity to pile up both points and assists.

**Original Line Publish**: 35.5

Let’s dive into three player props that catch our eye Thursday. Be sure to[check out the NBA betting trends page](https://www.lineups.com/nba/odds/betting-trends/)for odds, data and insights on every game.

## **Best NBA Thursday Props**

*   Darius Acuff Over 27.5 Points + Assists
*   Jarrett Allen Over 21.5 Points + Rebounds
*   Ja Morant Over 21.5 Points + Assists

### **Darius Acuff (Kings vs. Pelicans) Over Points + Assists**

Acuff has been thrust right into the fire from the onset of his career, and the rookie seventh overall pick has proven to be up to the challenge early on. Due to Ben Simmons’ unfortunate Achilles injury right before the start of the season, Acuff went from a projected second-unit member of the backcourt to the starting point guard. While Sacramento remains winless, the Arkansas product has roared back from a quiet Opening Night debut against the Clippers to supply 24.7 points, 5.3 assists and 2.7 rebounds across 35.3 minutes per contest over his subsequent three games.

Acuff is likely to see a fair share of Dejounte Murray defense in Thursday’s matchup, but that hasn’t proven prohibitive for opposing point guards thus far. New Orleans is allowing the fourth-highest offensive efficiency rating to the position (32.0), along with 50% shooting, including 43.3% from behind the arc. Additionally, the Pels went into Tuesday allowing the second-most points (32.0) and second-most assists (11.7) per game to the position, and Acuff also boasts a team-high 32.2% usage rate and averages 26.6 points + assists per 36 minutes with Keegan Murray (out, ankle) off the floor.

**Original Line Publish:** 27.5

### **Jarrett Allen (Cavaliers vs. Hawks) Over Points + Rebounds**

Allen is already churning out his usual production early this season, averaging 13.3 points and 9.0 rebounds per contest over his first four games. The veteran big man just pulled down a season-high 12 rebounds Wednesday night against the Wizards, the third straight game in which Allen had increased his production on the glass over that of the prior contest. Thursday marks the third game in four nights for both the Cavs and Hawks, but Allen is averaging a modest 27.7 minutes per game and shouldn’t be too fatigued this early in the season.

The opposing Hawks make for good targets on paper as well. Atlanta has been particularly vulnerable against centers early, as they went into Wednesday’s loss to the Heat already allowing the third-highest offensive efficiency rating (37.5), the second-highest shooting percentage (67.9) and the seventh-most points (23.8) per game to the position. Bam Adebayo then proceeded to trample Atlanta for 24 points and nine rebounds, and Bobby Portis added another 11 points and nine boards off the bench.

Finally, it’s worth noting the Hawks are allowing the seventh-most points in the paint per game (52.8), including 60.0 over the last three, while Allen is scoring 60.4% of his points in that part of the floor.

**Original Line Publish**: 21.5

### **Ja Morant (Trail Blazers vs. Grizzlies) Over Points + Assists**

Morant has been a solid fit early in his Trail Blazers tenure, meshing well alongside Damian Lillard in the backcourt while averaging 22.6 points + assists across 26.7 minutes per game. Perhaps as encouraging as anything regarding his overall body of work is his career-best 58.8% shooting, including 44.4% from three-point range.

Morant’s usage is unsurprisingly down from his Grizzlies days, as he’s now in a two-guard role and shares the floor with Damian Lillard and Deni Avdija. Nevertheless, it wouldn’t be surprising in the least to see Morant be a bit more involved Thursday in an intriguing matchup against his old Memphis teammates. The Grizzlies also make for a decent matchup on paper, as Memphis has surrendered the ninth-most points (26.0) to shooting guards, as well as a 93.3% success rate from the line to the position.

**Original Line Publish**: 21.5

# NBA First Basket Picks Today (Nov. 4): De’Aaron Fox

 Our NBA first basket pick for Nov. 4 is De’Aaron Fox at +600 as the Spurs host the Mavericks. Here’s why Fox is the play to score first tonight. 

![Image 20: De'Aaron Fox](https://www.lineups.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/2026-11-01T011220Z_865800005_MT1USATODAY30243852_RTRMADP_3_NBA-MINNESOTA-TIMBERWOLVES-AT-SAN-ANTONIO-SPURS-1-760x400.jpg)

Back like we never left. Not much exposition needed on this one without any games yesterday so we’ll dive right into it — tonight’s pick for NBA first basket is De’Aaron Fox at  odds.

## Challenge Update: 10 Miles in Jeans

Any consistent followers of our daily NBA picks know that we’re doing monthly challenges tied to each of these. To start the season, I promised that if I didn’t hit on at least three of my picks before Thanksgiving, I was going to film myself walking around the Brickell City Centre mall (Miami) in a dress. Thankfully, we put ourselves in a good position by hitting three of our first nine picks.

So lucky for you guys, we can get into some double trouble. I won’t be making these picks without stakes, so let’s fast-track to the next challenge.

> **Here’s my challenge:** If I don’t hit two **more** first basket picks by December 4, then I will run 10 miles in jeans and document every mile. Every mile where I don’t break 9:30 minutes adds another mile to the challenge.

## Why De’Aaron Fox For NBA First Basket vs. the Mavericks

The efficiency is what sells me on Fox at  odds. Through seven games, Fox is shooting 58.2% from the field (39-of-67) and has scored in double figures every night. He’s getting to his spots early in possessions, attacking downhill and pulling up from the midrange. Those are exactly the looks that show up on a team’s first trip down the floor.

The matchup helps too. Dallas will be without **Dereck Lively II** (foot), its best rim protector, which should open up the paint for a guard who lives there. On top of that, Victor Wembanyama gives San Antonio the edge on the opening tip, and when the Spurs get the first possession, the ball is usually in Fox’s hands.

The risk is obvious: this Spurs team has plenty of options, from Wemby to Dylan Harper (season-high 25 vs. Minnesota) to Stephon Castle. But at +600, we’re getting a fair price on the most efficient guard on the floor with a soft interior to attack.
